# Experiment 3, E3.2: span length, from one word to natural text

Run `20261015-223621_exp3e32`. TL attention at the query (position 32); HF cross-check on 10 rows per base class. 200 rows per base class; 36 variants × 12 passages per row. k ∈ [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24, 31]: the passage's last k tokens at 32 − k … 31. Late = layers 6–11, the mean over their heads. Attention is averaged over a row's passages before masses and labels; masks come from each contrast's treated arm (placebos: the masks of matched at that k).

Extraction: 172800 sequences in 1067 s (162 seq/s, TL only).

## Checks

| check | result |
|---|---|
| design (base outside written positions; filler holds no written id; no BOS; repeats only between positions holding one passage id, declared per passage, in every row) | PASS (asserted per variant and base class) |
| E3.2 layout (per-passage redraws only at collisions; nl / shuffled / matched / matched_b differ only on the span; shuffled keeps 31; matched keeps punctuation, one draw per word, nested; passage-rr only at 30–31; 2 × 2 cells differ only at 1–31 or 32; w from t's pool) | PASS (asserted per base class) |
| attention rows sum to 1 | PASS (atol 0.0001, asserted per batch; max |sum − 1| 4.7e-07) |
| determinism, rare (20 rows) | PASS (max |Δ| 0.0e+00) |
| TL vs HF, rare (10 rows, 4320 sequences) | PASS (max |Δ| 2.5e-05, argmax-key agreement 100.0000%) |
| placebo_8 switching heads ≤ 1, rare | PASS (0 argmax, 0 mass) |
| placebo_31 switching heads ≤ 1, rare | PASS (0 argmax, 0 mass) |
| determinism, common (20 rows) | PASS (max |Δ| 0.0e+00) |
| TL vs HF, common (10 rows, 4320 sequences) | PASS (max |Δ| 3.8e-05, argmax-key agreement 100.0000%) |
| placebo_8 switching heads ≤ 1, common | PASS (0 argmax, 0 mass) |
| placebo_31 switching heads ≤ 1, common | PASS (0 argmax, 0 mass) |

**All checks pass: yes.** The predictions count only if every check passes (exp3/PREDICTIONS_e32.md).
